# Where the demo library comes from

Every picture and clip in this directory is a real file from Wikimedia Commons,
under CC0 1.0 or CC BY (2.0, 3.0 or 4.0), checked on each file's own licence
metadata before download. CC BY asks for credit, which this table gives: the
author, the source page and the licence for every file. Nothing here is under a
share-alike, non-commercial or no-derivatives licence.

`scripts/build_fixture_library.py` builds the directory from `sources.json`:
pictures are scaled (1280 px for the ones a cut can reach, 500 px for the rest)
and stripped of metadata; clips are trimmed to five seconds at 720p. The
SHA-256 is of the file as shipped, so `tests/test_fixture_library.py` can prove
every file is the one credited here. `library.json` records what the fake
Immich serves for each file: the GPS and capture time from the file's own
metadata (home days moved by whole days into July 2025, the road trip
unmoved), and the face boxes the build pinned to the made-up household.

The household, its names and its story are a script in `tests/e2e/fake_library.py`,
not facts about these photographs or the people in them.
